- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** ATP-Hygiene Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Supermarket Food Safety Rapid Lab Testing
